We contribute with a purposefully designed three-stage training process - vision-language alignment, general visual instruction tuning, and preference optimization - together with a pt-PT-centric multimodal data mix combining curated and translated public datasets with novel datasets that address the near-total absence of European Portuguese multimodal resources. Our evaluation shows that AMALIA-VL establishes a strong baseline for open-source pt-PT LVLMs.We will release model weights, training data, and construction pipelines along with machine-translated pt-PT evaluation benchmarks to help democratize pt-PT LVLM development.

</details>


### [72] [ProductConsistency: Improving Product Identity Preservation in Instruction-Based Image Editing via SFT and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19103)
*Mukund Khanna,Raj Singh Yadav,Kunal Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对产品图像编辑中难以保持产品特征、品牌和文本细粒度一致性的问题，本文提出了ProductConsistency数据集和训练方法，在多个模型上实现了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于指令的图像编辑模型在产品中心化场景中，难以保持产品特征、品牌标识和文本等细粒度对象身份。这主要是由于缺乏专门针对产品图像编辑并约束文本保真度的数据集，导致该能力主要依赖模型的隐式学习。

Method: 构建了ProductConsistency数据集，包含87k样本的监督微调（SFT）数据集、869个独特产品图像的强化学习（RL）数据集和新的评估基准。提出循环一致性奖励，通过计算原始产品描述与编辑后图像生成的描述之间的语义相似度，来强化产品身份的保持。使用此数据集和奖励对Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev模型进行微调。

Result: 微调后的模型在OCR指标、感知指标以及基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的评估中均优于基线模型，表现出更强的产品一致性、文本渲染能力和整体视觉质量。其中，Qwen-Image-Edit-2511模型的字符错误率降低了5倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数据集和循环一致性训练方法能有效提升指令驱动图像编辑模型在产品中心化场景下的表现，特别是增强了对产品身份和文本元素的保持能力，为相关应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in instruction-based image editing have enabled models to perform complex visual edits from natural language instructions. However, in product-centric scenarios where preserving product features, branding, and textual elements are critical, current open and closed source models often struggle to maintain this fine-grained object identity. This issue is further compounded by the lack of datasets for instruction-based product image editing with text fidelity constraints, leaving it largely treated as an implicit capability of instruction-based image editing models.
  In this work, we introduce the ProductConsistency dataset which is designed to improve product-centric image editing. Our approach includes a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) dataset of 87k samples for product editing, a reinforcement learning (RL) dataset with 869 unique product images, and a new benchmark dataset, the ProductConsistency Benchmark, to allow rigorous and standardized evaluation of editing models. To guide RL training, we propose a Cyclic Consistency reward that enforces semantic preservation of product identity by using caption similarity between the original product description and captions generated from the edited image. We fine-tune both Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 and Flux.1-Kontext-dev using our dataset and demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models in OCR and Perceptual metrics, and MLLM-based evaluations as well, indicating stronger product consistency, text rendering, and overall visual quality; with the Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 model achieving a 5x reduction in the character error rate. The code and pipeline is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProductConsistency-6FCC/README.md

</details>


### [73] [Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset: A Historical Document Dataset for Offline Urdu Handwritten Text Recognition with CRNN-Based Baseline Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19139)
*Ramza Basharat,Muhammad Usman Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对乌尔都语手写文本识别（UHTR）的研究不足问题，创建了首个专门的历史Katib手写数据集UKHD，并评估了CRNN混合模型，发现CNN-BGRU-CTC模型表现最佳，旨在推动相关识别系统的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语手写文本识别（UHTR）研究相对滞后，主要原因在于其独特的脚本特性（如连笔书写）以及缺乏标准的基准数据集。为了推进该领域的研究，需要构建专门的数据集并评估有效的识别模型。

Method: 1. **数据集构建**：创建了乌尔都语Katib手写数据集（UKHD），这是首个专门从历史Katib（书法家）撰写的材料中离线收集的乌尔都语手写文本行数据集，涵盖了Nastalique书法风格中的多种平笔书写变体。2. **模型评估**：评估了不同的基于CRNN（卷积循环神经网络）的混合模型，以寻找针对乌尔都语Katib手写识别（UKHR）的最优架构。

Result: 在所有被评估的模型中，CNN-BGRU-CTC（卷积神经网络-双向门控循环单元-连接时序分类）模型表现出更稳健的性能，取得了较低的字符错误率（CER）和词错误率（WER）。

Conclusion: 本研究通过提供专用数据集和性能优越的模型，旨在支持和鼓励研究社区开发稳健的乌尔都语手写文献识别系统，以促进其保护与研究。

Abstract: Automatic Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) is inherently a challenging task, and its complexity is further increased when dealing with cursive scripts. Although significant efforts have been made on various cursive scripts, research regarding Urdu Handwritten Text Recognition (UHTR) has been relatively limited. This lag of research is primarily due to the unique challenges posed by its script, and the scarcity and unavailability of benchmark datasets. Therefore, to advance research in UHTR, this study presents a specialized real dataset called the Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset (UKHD). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first offline Urdu handwritten text lines dataset specifically curated from the materials written by Katibs in historical times. It encompasses a diverse range of flat nib writing variations in the Nastalique calligraphic style. Additionally, the effectiveness of different CRNN-based hybrid models has been evaluated to identify the optimal architecture for Urdu Katib Handwriting Recognition (UKHR). Among the analyzed models, the CNN-BGRU-CTC model showed more robust performance, with low Character Error Rate (CER) and Word Error Rate (WER). This research work aims to support and encourage the research community in developing a robust recognition system for preserving Urdu handwritten literature.

</details>


### [74] [Hand-4DGS: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for 4D Hand Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19156)
*Jeongmin Bae,Seoha Kim,Marc Pollefeys,Mahdi Rad,Youngjung Uh,Taein Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了首个用于从第一人称视角视频直接重建动态4D手部的前馈框架（Hand-4DGS），实现了快速推理（~60 FPS）和强大的泛化能力，无需昂贵的3D标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D手部重建对于AR/VR和AI眼镜等下一代计算平台至关重要。现有研究主要关注多视角3D手部重建或4D人体重建，而从第一人称视角进行4D手部重建仍面临快速头部运动、手部动态快速变化、严重遮挡以及单视角观测固有模糊性等挑战。

Method: 提出Hand-4DGS前馈框架。该框架采用网格引导的表示法作为结构先验，并利用时间卷积来建模动态运动。结合了前馈网络的泛化能力和通过高斯溅射（Gaussian Splatting）进行有效2D图像监督的方法。

Result: 在H2O和ARCTIC两个具有挑战性的第一人称视角数据集上进行了评估，结果表明该方法在性能上显著优于基线方法。框架实现了快速的推理速度（约60 FPS），并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Hand-4DGS框架有效解决了从第一人称视角视频进行动态4D手部重建的核心挑战，为下一代人机交互平台（如AR/VR）提供了一种高效、可泛化的解决方案，且该方案无需依赖昂贵的3D手部姿态真值数据。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D hand reconstruction from egocentric videos is essential for next-generation computing platforms such as AR/VR and AI glasses. Despite its importance, most prior works focus either on multi-view 3D hand reconstruction or on 4D human body reconstruction. Egocentric 4D hand reconstruction remains challenging due to fast head motion, rapid hand dynamics, severe occlusions, and inherent ambiguity from single-view observations. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand-4DGS, the first feed-forward framework for reconstructing dynamic 4D hands directly from egocentric videos, enabling both fast (~60 FPS) inference and strong generalization. Our approach incorporates a mesh-guided representation for structural priors and temporal convolutions to model dynamic motion. We evaluate our framework on two challenging egocentric datasets, H2O and ARCTIC, and demonstrate significant improvements over baselines. Our method benefits from the generalization capability of feed-forward networks and effective 2D image supervision through Gaussian splatting, without requiring expensive 3D hand pose ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [75] [When AUC Misleads: Polarization-Aware Evaluation of Deepfake Detectors under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19184)
*Dat Nguyen,Cosmin Radoi,Romain Hermary,Marcella Astrid,Nesryne Mejri,Enjie Ghorbel,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对深度伪造检测领域，指出现有评估指标（AUC）无法反映真实场景中检测器面对混合数据源和伪影类型的挑战，因此提出一种新的评估指标——跨数据集AUC（Cross-AUC），通过结合预测极化度（用Wasserstein距离量化）来更真实地评估检测器的泛化能力和鲁棒性，并在七个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI（如扩散模型和换脸工具）的发展，深度伪造技术已导致金融欺诈和非自愿色情等现实危害，因此深度伪造检测成为研究热点。现有方法旨在提升对未见操纵的泛化能力，但其评估方式（分别在多个数据集上计算AUC）未能模拟真实场景——检测器实际面对的是混合数据源和多种伪影类型的组合，这导致评估结果不具代表性，无法有效反映模型在实际应用中的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者提出一种新的评估指标：跨数据集AUC（Cross-AUC）。该指标通过计算每个领域（数据集）的AUC平均值，并引入预测极化程度的度量来考虑模型对领域偏移的鲁棒性。具体而言，极化程度通过类别（真实与伪造）得分分布之间的Wasserstein距离进行量化，从而综合评估模型在多个领域下的表现稳定性和泛化能力。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的实验表明，Cross-AUC能够更真实、更全面地评估深度伪造检测器在领域偏移条件下的泛化能力，并且该指标具有较好的可解释性，能够帮助分析模型性能下降的具体原因（例如由于预测分布的极化现象）。

Conclusion: Cross-AUC是一个实用且可解释的评估指标，它改进了深度伪造检测模型的评估方式，能够更贴近真实场景地衡量模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性，有助于推动该领域研究向更实际应用的方向发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI, such as diffusion models and face-swapping tools, have enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfakes, leading to real-world harms including financial fraud and non-consensual explicit content. In response, deepfake detection has become an active research area, with recent methods increasingly focusing on improving generalization to unseen manipulations. This is typically evaluated using the Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) measured separately across multiple datasets. However, such an evaluation fails to reflect real-world scenarios where detectors face a mixture of data sources and varying artifact types. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel metric, Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC) that averages per-domain AUCs with a measure of prediction polarization for taking into account the robustness to domain shift. The polarization extent is quantified by the Wasserstein Distance between class score distributions. Cross-AUC not only assesses the generalization capabilities of deepfake detectors under domain shifts more realistically, but it is also interpretable as it better explains the reason behind a drop in performance. Experiments performed on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate its practical relevance.

</details>


### [76] [Moebius: 0.2B Lightweight Image Inpainting Framework with 10B-Level Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19195)
*Kangsheng Duan,Ziyang Xu,Wenyu Liu,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Moebius的高效轻量级图像修复框架，它在保持与工业级大型基础模型相当的高质量修复能力的同时，大幅降低了计算成本和推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级的10B参数规模基础模型在图像修复任务中表现优异，但其巨大的计算成本严重阻碍了实际部署。构建针对特定任务的高度优化的专用模型是一个有前途的方案，但极端的结构压缩不可避免地会引发严重的表示瓶颈。本文的动机就是为了解决这一矛盾，在保持高质量修复能力的前提下实现高效的模型压缩与加速。

Method: 本文提出了Moebius框架。其核心方法是系统性地重构扩散模型主干网络，引入了一个新的基础模块——Local-λ Mix Interaction (LλMI) block。该模块包含Local-λ和Interactive-λ两个子模块，能够将空间上下文和全局语义先验优雅地总结到固定大小的线性矩阵中，在大幅减少参数的同时保留复杂的潜在交互。此外，为了充分释放这个高度紧凑架构的表示潜力，本文采用了一种自适应的多粒度蒸馏策略进行协同优化。该策略严格在潜在空间操作以避免昂贵的像素空间解码，并通过动态平衡多个基于梯度的损失函数来实现高保真度的对齐。

Result: 在自然图像和人像基准测试上的大量实验表明，通过最优的架构设计与蒸馏策略的协同作用，Moebius能够达到甚至超越10B级工业通用模型FLUX.1-Fill-Dev的生成质量。值得注意的是，Moebius仅使用了不到其2%的参数（0.22B vs. 11.9B），同时将总推理时间加速了15倍以上，为高保真图像修复设立了新的效率标准。

Conclusion: Moebius框架通过创新的LλMI模块结构设计和自适应的蒸馏策略，成功解决了轻量化模型中常见的表示瓶颈问题。它在显著降低参数量和推理成本的同时，实现了媲美甚至超越大型工业模型的图像修复质量，证明了构建高效、高性能任务专用模型路线的可行性，为推动图像修复技术的实际应用部署提供了关键的技术方案。

Abstract: While 10B-level industrial foundation models have pushed the boundaries of image inpainting, their prohibitive computational costs severely hinder practical deployment. Constructing a highly optimized task-specific specialist offers a promising solution; however, extreme structural compression inevitably triggers a severe representation bottleneck. To conquer this, we propose Moebius, a highly efficient lightweight inpainting framework. We systematically reconstruct the diffusion backbone by introducing the Local-$λ$ Mix Interaction ($LλMI$) block. Comprising Local-$λ$ and Interactive-$λ$ modules, it elegantly summarizes spatial contexts and global semantic priors into fixed-size linear matrices, preserving complex latent interactions while drastically shedding parameters. Furthermore, to unlock the full representational capacity of this highly compact architecture, we synergistically pair it with an adaptive multi-granularity distillation strategy. Operating strictly within the latent space to avoid expensive pixel-space decoding, this strategy dynamically balances multiple gradient-based losses to achieve high-fidelity alignment. Extensive experiments across natural and portrait benchmarks demonstrate that this optimal synergy enables Moebius to rival or even surpass the generation quality of the 10B-level industrial generalist FLUX.1-Fill-Dev. Remarkably, Moebius achieves this using less than 2\% of the parameters (0.22B vs. 11.9B) while delivering a $>15\times$ acceleration in total inference time, setting a new efficiency standard for high-fidelity inpainting. Project page at https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius.

</details>


### [77] [ROSA-TFormer: A Radar-Optical Sensor-Aware Temporal Transformer for Pinus sylvestris Plantation Classification in Northern Shaanxi Using GEE-Derived Sentinel-1/2 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19204)
*Nengbo Zhang,Chang sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为ROSA-TFormer的雷达-光学传感器感知时间Transformer模型，利用Sentinel-1/2时间序列数据，实现了对陕北地区樟子松（Pinus sylvestris var. mongolica）人工林的高精度点级分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确识别樟子松人工林对于监测陕北地区的造林质量和生态恢复至关重要。现有的分类方法在融合多源（雷达与光学）传感器数据和捕捉物候时间序列特征方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出了ROSA-TFormer模型，该模型基于Google Earth Engine生成的Sentinel-1/2时间序列数据。模型架构包含独立的SAR和光学嵌入分支、一个传感器感知门控机制以及时间注意力池化模块，旨在捕捉多源数据的季节性特征并实现有效融合。

Result: 在月度和半月度点级数据集上进行的实验表明，ROSA-TFormer取得了优异的分类性能。在HalfMonth-dataBig数据集上，总体精度达到99.67%，宏平均F1分数为99.56%，樟子松的F1分数为98.91%。空间分块验证和消融实验结果进一步证明了雷达-光学时间融合及传感器感知建模的有效性。

Conclusion: ROSA-TFormer模型在点级樟子松人工林分类上展现出强大潜力，证实了多源时序数据融合的优势。然而，研究也指出，该方法仍需在更广泛的区域（如wall-to-wall制图）进行验证。

Abstract: Accurate identification of Pinus sylvestris var. mongolica plantations is important for monitoring afforestation quality and ecological restoration in northern Shaanxi. This paper proposes ROSA-TFormer, a radar-optical sensor-aware temporal Transformer for P. sylvestris classification using Sentinel-1/2 time-series data generated on Google Earth Engine. The model integrates separate SAR and optical embedding branches, a sensor-aware gate, and temporal attention pooling to capture multi-source seasonal features. Experiments on monthly and half-month point-level datasets show that ROSA-TFormer achieves strong classification performance, with 99.67% overall accuracy, 99.56% macro F1, and 98.91% P. sylvestris F1 on the HalfMonth-dataBig dataset. Spatial block validation and ablation results further indicate the effectiveness of radar-optical temporal fusion and sensor-aware modeling. The results demonstrate the potential of ROSA-TFormer for point-level P. sylvestris plantation classification, while broader wall-to-wall validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [78] [GUMP-Net: An interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19215)
*Liheng Wang,Yinghui Zhang,Licheng Zhang,Hailin Xu,Qiyong Cao,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GUMP-Net，一种结合改进测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络的可解释数据驱动智能算法，用于多类骨盆分割，在小样本情况下性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨盆分割是骨盆骨折精准智能诊疗、手术规划与导航中最重要且基础的研究问题之一。

Method: 结合改进的测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络，设计三个网络模块构成整体分割框架：目标检测模块实现自动水平集初始化、边缘检测器模块学习解剖感知的边缘检测函数、迭代模块进行深度水平集演化。

Result: 在小规模训练数据条件下，相较于现有先进方法，该方法展现出更精确、稳健且一致的分割性能。在骨盆及踝关节数据集上的大量实验验证了算法的合理性与有效性，并证实其可扩展至其他解剖结构。

Conclusion: 该算法不仅为复杂骨折复位提供了高效的分割方法，也为理解深度学习分割提供了可解释的几何视角。

Abstract: Pelvic segmentation is one of the most important and fundamental research problems in precise and intelligent diagnosis and treatment, as well as surgical planning and navigation for pelvic fractures. By combining an improved geodesic active contour model with deep neural networks, we propose GUMP-Net, an interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation, in which three network modules are designed to constitute the overall segmentation framework together: the object detection module for automatic level set initialization, the edge detector module for learning an anatomy-aware edge detector function and the iteration module for deep level set evolution. Leveraging the advantages of level set representation and deep learning, GUMP-Net shows more accurate, robust and consistent segmentation performance, especially in small training data situation, compared to the state-of-the-art methods. Extensive experiments on pelvic datasets demonstrate the rationality and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm. Further experiments extended to ankle dataset indicate broader applications to other anatomies. The proposed algorithm not only provides an efficient segmentation method for complex fracture reduction, but also gives an interpretable geometric perspective for understanding deep learning segmentation.

</details>


### [79] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-I: Spectral Geometry Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19249)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出Transformer几何观测框架(TGO)来分析Vision Transformer的表示几何，发现训练过程中表示维度利用率增加且方差在维度间重新分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers已在计算机视觉中广泛应用，但其维度和表示几何的基本原理尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 使用Transformer几何观测框架(TGO-I)，在ImageNet-100数据集上训练ViT-Small/16模型，分析有效秩、稳定秩、参与比、谱熵、谱平坦度、谱各向异性、协方差结构、特征谱和奇异值谱。

Result: 研究发现训练过程中维度利用率持续增加，伴随各向异性降低、谱熵增加、参与比提高以及特征谱逐渐变平。与直觉相反，训练并未将信息集中到少数主导方向，而是在表示维度间重新分配方差，这在最终CLS token表示中尤为明显，表现出最高有效维度和最低各向异性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Vision Transformer训练过程中表示几何的演化规律，表明模型倾向于在维度间重新分配方差而非集中信息，这对理解ViT的表示学习机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) and their success across numerous computer vision applications, the fundamental understanding of their dimensional and representational geometry remains relatively underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO), a systematic framework of experiments and analysis pipelines designed to investigate the representational geometry and dynamics of Vision Transformers. TGO-I, the first installment of the framework, focuses on the spectral geometry of ViT representations. Using a ViT-Small/16 model trained on ImageNet-100, we analyze Effective Rank, Stable Rank, Participation Ratio, Spectral Entropy, Spectral Flatness, Spectral Anisotropy, covariance structure, eigenspectra, and singular value spectra throughout training. Our results reveal a consistent increase in dimensional utilization, accompanied by decreasing anisotropy, increasing spectral entropy, increasing participation ratio, and progressively flatter eigenspectra. Contrary to the common intuition that training should concentrate information into a small number of dominant directions, we observe a progressive redistribution of variance across representational dimensions. This phenomenon is particularly pronounced in the final CLS token representation, which exhibits the highest effective dimensionality and lowest anisotropy within the network.

</details>


### [80] [A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Text-Rich Images from GPT-Image-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19259)
*Yijin Wang,Shuyi Wang,Wenhan Zhang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多领域基准用于检测由GPT Image 2生成的文本丰富图像，评估发现现有检测器性能高度依赖图像类别且对压缩敏感，表明需要文本和布局感知的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测基准主要关注以物体为中心的图像，对文本语义和布局组织至关重要的场景覆盖不足，而随着多模态模型合成逼真文本能力的提升，检测此类文本丰富图像对数字信任和内容真实性构成重要挑战。

Method: 构建了包含8602张图像、覆盖商业海报、信息图表、学术海报、收据、表格和UI截图六个类别的多领域基准；在零样本设置下评估了五种代表性的AI生成图像检测器，分析了其整体、分类别及后处理（如JPEG压缩）的鲁棒性；此外，还使用多模态视觉语言模型进行了探索性评估。

Result: 检测器性能高度依赖领域：在某些类别上表现良好的方法在其他类别上往往失败；即使是性能最强的传统检测器也对JPEG压缩表现出严重敏感性；多模态视觉语言模型在结构化格式上展现出潜力，但也存在局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了开发能够感知文本和布局的现代AI生成图像检测方法的必要性，并为此发布了一个新的基准数据集。

Abstract: Text-rich images often contain privacy-sensitive, transactional, or decision-relevant information. As recent multimodal image generation models become increasingly capable of synthesizing realistic textual content and structured visual designs, detecting AI-generated text-rich images has become an important challenge for digital trust and content authenticity. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on object-centric images and provide limited coverage of scenarios where textual semantics and layout organization are central. In this paper, we introduce a multi-domain benchmark for detecting text-rich images generated by OpenAI's GPT Image 2. The benchmark contains 8,602 images across six representative categories: commercial posters, infographics, academic posters, receipts, tables, and UI screenshots. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five representative AI-generated image detectors in a zero-shot setting and analyze their overall, category-wise, and post-processing robustness. Our results show that detector performance is highly domain-dependent: methods that perform well in some categories often fail on others, and even the strongest conventional detector exhibits severe sensitivity to JPEG compression. We further conduct an exploratory evaluation with a multimodal vision-language model, revealing both its promise and its limitations on structured formats. These findings highlight the need for text- and layout-aware detection methods for modern AI-generated images. Our dataset is released at XXX.

</details>


### [81] [A Unified Framework for Efficient Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering: Adapting Dual, Hybrid, and Encoder-Decoder Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19277)
*Timothy Agboada,Shikha Chandel,Yadav Raj Ghimire,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对遥感视觉问答（RSVQA）的领域挑战，提出了一种基于参数高效微调（PEFT）的RS Adapter方法，并比较了其在CLIP、BLIP和FLAVA三种视觉语言模型架构上的应用效果，发现混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理和检索任务上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感影像因其高分辨率、多尺度目标分布和语义复杂性，对视觉问答（VQA）提出了独特挑战。现有通用基础模型因存在显著的领域差异且完全微调的计算成本过高，难以直接应用于RSVQA。本研究旨在探索一种能高效适应遥感领域的模型微调策略。

Method: 研究提出了一种统一的“架构手术”流程：将轻量级的瓶颈适配器（bottleneck adapters）注入到冻结的骨干网络（backbones）的注意力层和MLP层中，实现参数高效微调（PEFT）。该方法在双编码器CLIP、编码器-解码器BLIP和混合架构FLAVA三种模型上进行了实施和比较，可训练参数均少于5%。

Result: 在高分辨率RSVQA-x数据集上的实验表明，所有经过适配的模型均能收敛。其中，混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理和检索能力的平衡上优于其对应的单模态模型。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了在灾难评估和城市监测等资源受限场景下，进行高效视觉问答的新基线。FLAVA结合RS Adapter的方案为遥感领域的多模态任务提供了一种有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) in the Remote Sensing (RS) domain presents unique challenges due to the high resolution, multi scale object distribution, and semantic complexity of aerial imagery. While general domain Foundation Models have achieved remarkable success, their direct application to RSVQA is hindered by massive domain shifts and the computationally prohibitive nature of full fine tuning. This study presents a comparative analysis of RS Adapter, a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) strategy, applied across three distinct Vision Language Model (VLM) architectures: the Dual Encoder CLIP, the Encoder Decoder BLIP, and the Hybrid FLAVA. We introduce a unified architectural surgery pipeline that injects lightweight bottleneck adapters into the attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with less than 5 percent of trainable parameters. Experimental results on the high resolution RSVQA x dataset demonstrate that while all adapted models achieve convergence, the Hybrid FLAVA architecture offers a superior balance of multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities compared to its unimodal counterparts. Our findings establish a new baseline for resource efficient VQA in disaster assessment and urban monitoring.

</details>


### [82] [Confidence is Not Reliability: Rethinking MC Dropout in Brain Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19300)
*Xin Ci Wong,Duygu Sarikaya,Kieran Zucker,Marc De Kamps,Nishant Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在多参数MRI胶质瘤分割中，使用蒙特卡洛Dropout进行体素级不确定性估计以识别临床关键子区域分割错误的可行性。研究发现，MC Dropout在保持分割精度的同时，能提供良好的不确定性与错误对齐（AUROC≈0.97），但这种全局对齐可能掩盖区域特异性问题。一个模型（UNet-Res）在增强肿瘤子区域表现出严重的校准错误，这是标准Dice和AUROC指标无法检测的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数MRI中的胶质瘤分割是治疗规划的关键环节。然而，传统基于重叠的指标（如Dice分数）无法揭示分割模型在治疗关键子区域可能发生的静默失效，这对患者安全构成风险。本研究旨在探究体素级不确定性估计（通过MC Dropout）能否可靠地识别这些关键区域的错误，以及标准报告指标是否足以检测校准失效。

Method: 研究采用了实证双模型案例研究，在126名BraTS21患者数据上评估了两个模型：一个高性能的预训练SegResNet和一个本地训练的UNet-Res。方法上，应用MC Dropout进行不确定性估计，评估其对分割精度的影响（ΔDice），计算不确定性与错误的对齐程度（熵AUROC），并利用熵进行患者分层。此外，重点分析了区域特异性的校准情况，使用了期望校准误差等指标。

Result: 主要结果：1) MC Dropout在应用时对分割精度的影响极小（|ΔDice| <0.01）。2) 不确定性与分割错误具有很强的对齐关系（熵AUROC约0.97），表明不确定性能够正确地将错误体素排在正确体素之前。3) 基于熵的患者分层识别出一个高不确定性亚组，其分割性能显著较低（全肿瘤Dice中位数0.835 vs 0.925），表明不确定性可作为实用的分诊信号。4) 关键发现：尽管全局AUROC相似，但UNet-Res模型在最具临床意义的“增强肿瘤”子区域表现出严重的校准错误，其熵值极低（0.054），期望校准误差高达0.915，对应的Dice仅为0.714。这种区域性的校准失效无法通过标准的Dice和AUROC报告检测出来。

Conclusion: 研究结论是，强不确定性与错误的对齐（如高AUROC）对于临床安全部署是必要的，但并不充分。当为临床应用选择模型时，必须结合子区域特异性的校准评估来审查模型，以避免在关键解剖结构上出现未被发现的过度自信错误。标准报告指标存在盲区，需要更细致的评估方法。

Abstract: Glioma segmentation in multiparametric MRI is a critical component of treatment planning. A segmentation model that fails silently on treatment-critical sub-regions represents a patient safety risk that overlap-based metrics such as Dice scores cannot expose. We ask whether voxel-level uncertainty estimation via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout can reliably identify segmentation errors in clinically critical sub-regions, and whether calibration failure modes are detectable from standard reporting metrics alone. In an empirical two-model case study on 126 BraTS21 patients, we evaluate a high-performance pretrained SegResNet and a locally trained UNet with residual units (UNet-Res). MC dropout preserved segmentation accuracy ($|Δ\text{Dice}|$ $<0.01$) while achieving strong uncertainty-error alignment (AUROC for entropy (H) $\approx$0.97), indicating uncertainty correctly ranks erroneous voxels above correct ones. Entropy-based patient stratification identified a high-uncertainty subgroup with substantially lower segmentation performance (median whole-tumour Dice $0.835$ vs. $0.925$), supporting uncertainty as a practical triage signal. However, global alignment can mask important region-specific differences. Despite similar AUROC, UNet-Res exhibited near-zero enhancing tumour entropy ($0.054$) and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of $0.915$, with a Dice of only $0.714$, indicating severely miscalibrated confidence on the most clinically critical sub-region, a failure mode invisible to standard Dice and AUROC reporting. These findings demonstrate that strong uncertainty-error alignment is necessary but insufficient for clinical safety: sub-region-specific calibration assessment must accompany AUROC evaluation when selecting models for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [83] [NeuMesh++: Towards Versatile and Efficient Volumetric Editing with Disentangled Neural Mesh-based Implicit Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19316)
*Chong Bao,Yuan Li,Bangbang Yang,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui,Yinda Zhang,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuMesh++，一种基于网格的神经辐射场表示方法，将解耦的几何、纹理和语义编码于网格顶点，从而支持一系列高效且全面的编辑功能，包括网格引导的几何编辑、指定的纹理编辑及语义引导编辑，并在真实与合成数据集上验证了其优越的表示质量与编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经隐式渲染方法在编辑场景时功能有限，通常仅支持刚性变换或特定类别的编辑操作，缺乏灵活性和通用性。因此，需要一种能够支持更丰富、更精细编辑功能的神经渲染表示与编辑框架。

Method: 提出一种新颖的网格表示，将神经辐射场（NeRF）的几何、纹理与语义代码解耦并编码于网格顶点。关键技术包括：1. 局部空间参数化以提升渲染质量与训练稳定性；2. 顶点上的可学习修改颜色以提高纹理编辑保真度；3. 空间感知优化策略以实现精确纹理编辑；4. 语义辅助区域选择以降低隐式场编辑的标注成本。

Result: 在多个真实与合成数据集上的大量实验及编辑示例表明，所提方法在渲染质量与编辑能力（包括几何、纹理和语义编辑）方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: NeuMesh++通过创新的网格表示与一系列配套编辑技术，显著增强了神经隐式渲染的编辑功能与实用性，为3D场景的精细编辑提供了有效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently neural implicit rendering techniques have evolved rapidly and demonstrated significant advantages in novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, existing neural rendering methods for editing purposes offer limited functionalities, e.g., rigid transformation and category-specific editing. In this paper, we present a novel mesh-based representation by encoding the neural radiance field with disentangled geometry, texture, and semantic codes on mesh vertices, which empowers a set of efficient and comprehensive editing functionalities, including mesh-guided geometry editing, designated texture editing with texture swapping, filling and painting operations, and semantic-guided editing. To this end, we develop several techniques including a novel local space parameterization to enhance rendering quality and training stability, a learnable modification color on vertex to improve the fidelity of texture editing, a spatial-aware optimization strategy to realize precise texture editing, and a semantic-aided region selection to ease the laborious annotation of implicit field editing. Extensive experiments and editing examples on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method on representation quality and editing ability. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/

</details>


### [84] [Beyond the Current Observation: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models in Controllable Non-Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19338)
*Shengyuan Ding,Xilin Wei,Xinyu Fang,Haodong Duan,Dahua Lin,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RNG-Bench是一个专门评估多模态基础模型在非马尔可夫环境中重建过去观测能力的基准测试。它通过两个游戏隔离重建能力，发现前沿模型仍面临挑战，主要瓶颈是遗忘而非决策，微调可改善性能且无害迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法有效隔离模型的“隐状态重建”能力：要么暴露完整状态，要么将重建与其他技能（如规划）混淆，或仅在回合结束后测试回忆。因此，需要一个专门的基准来评估模型在多步交互中实时重建并利用过往观测的能力。

Method: 提出RNG-Bench，包含两个互补游戏：1. Matching Pairs（记忆短暂显示的卡牌位置）。2. 3D Maze（整合自我中心视角构建空间地图）。两者均在统一框架下运行，控制三个难度轴（网格大小、视觉图案、观测模态）。引入“头对头决斗”协议以控制实例级方差，并提出“Memory Gap”指标以区分“遗忘”和“决策不佳”。

Result: 1. 最难配置需要约128K token上下文和每回合350张图像输入，前沿多模态大模型（MLLMs）远未饱和。2. Memory Gap分析表明，残余错误主要源于对先前观测的“遗忘”，而非次优决策。3. 在最优策略轨迹和经过筛选的模型演示上微调Qwen3.5-9B，不仅能提升RNG-Bench性能，还能迁移至现有基准，且不损害通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: RNG-Bench成功隔离并评估了基础模型的非马尔可夫观测重建能力。研究表明，当前前沿模型的主要瓶颈是记忆遗忘而非决策能力，且通过针对性微调可有效改善这一能力，并展现出良好的迁移性。该基准为评估和改进模型的长期记忆与状态重建能力提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Deploying multimodal foundation models as closed-loop policies increasingly requires conditioning actions on observations that are no longer visible. However, existing benchmarks either expose the full state, conflate hidden-state reconstruction with other agent skills, or test recall only after an episode has ended. We introduce RNG-Bench (Reconstructive Non-Markov Games), a benchmark suite designed to isolate a base model's ability to reconstruct past observations and act on them during multi-step interaction. RNG-Bench includes two complementary games: Matching Pairs, where card identities briefly revealed at specific locations must later be recalled, and 3D Maze, where egocentric views must be integrated into a spatial map. Both games are evaluated under a unified harness with three controlled difficulty axes: grid size, visual pattern, and observation modality. The benchmark further introduces a head-to-head duel protocol to control for instance-level variance and a Memory Gap metric that disentangles forgetting from poor action selection. The hardest configurations require contexts of roughly 128K tokens and 350 image inputs per episode, and remain far from saturated by frontier MLLMs. Memory Gap analysis shows that most residual errors stem from forgetting earlier observations rather than from suboptimal decision making. Finally, fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal-policy rollouts and filtered model demonstrations improves performance on RNG-Bench and transfers to existing benchmarks without degrading general multimodal capability.

</details>


### [85] [Native Active Perception as Reasoning for Omni-Modal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19341)
*Zhenghao Xing,Ruiyang Xu,Yuxuan Wang,Jinzheng He,Ziyang Ma,Qize Yang,Yunfei Chu,Jin Xu,Junyang Lin,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniAgent是首个原生全模态智能体，用于长视频理解。它将视频理解构建为基于POMDP的迭代“观察-思考-行动”循环，按需执行动作，将视听线索选择性提炼为持久文本记忆，从而将推理复杂度与原始视频长度解耦，在多个基准测试中达到开源模型的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动模型采用“观看全部”范式，无论查询难度如何都均匀处理所有帧，导致计算成本随视频时长线性增长。虽然交互式框架已出现，但它们通常依赖全局预扫描，且上下文成本仍与视频长度成正比。因此，需要一种能够实现按需处理、将推理复杂度与原始视频时长有效解耦的新方法。

Method: 1. 将视频理解形式化为基于部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）的迭代“观察-思考-行动”（Observation-Thought-Action）循环，通过智能体执行按需动作，将视听线索选择性提炼为持久文本记忆。
2. 提出智能体监督微调（Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning）：通过带有双重质量控制的最佳N轨迹合成（best-of-N trajectory synthesis）来引导原生主动感知。
3. 提出智能体强化学习（Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA）：利用回合级熵（turn-level entropy）来引导信用分配（credit assignment），使其倾向于关键的发现回合，从而优化决策过程。

Result: 1. OmniAgent表现出正向的测试时缩放（test-time scaling）特性，即随着推理回合数的增加，性能持续提升，这验证了主动感知策略的有效性。
2. 在包括VideoMME和LVBench在内的十个基准测试上，OmniAgent在开源模型中达到了最先进的性能。
3. 在LVBench基准上，7B参数的OmniAgent智能体以50.5%的准确率，超越了10倍规模的Qwen2.5-VL-72B模型（47.3%）。

Conclusion: OmniAgent通过基于POMDP的迭代决策框架和主动感知策略，成功地将长视频理解的推理复杂度与视频时长解耦。其提出的智能体监督微调和强化学习方法有效提升了模型的决策能力。实验证明，该模型不仅性能优越，还具备良好的测试时缩放能力，为长视频理解提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Passive models for long video understanding typically rely on a "watch-it-all" paradigm, processing frames uniformly regardless of query difficulty, causing computational cost to grow with video duration. Although interactive frameworks have emerged, they often rely on global pre-scanning, and their context cost still scales with video length. We propose OmniAgent, the first native omni-modal agent that formulates video understanding as a POMDP-based iterative Observation-Thought-Action cycle. OmniAgent executes on-demand actions to selectively distill audio-visual cues into a persistent textual memory, effectively decoupling reasoning complexity from raw video duration. To operationalize this, we introduce (1) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning to bootstrap native active perception via best-of-N trajectory synthesis with dual-stage quality control, and (2) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA (Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage), which leverages turn-level entropy to steer credit assignment toward pivotal discovery turns. Crucially, OmniAgent exhibits positive test-time scaling, where performance improves as the number of reasoning turns increases, validating the efficacy of active perception. Empirical results across ten benchmarks (e.g., VideoMME, LVBench) demonstrate that OmniAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Notably, on LVBench, our 7B agent outperforms the 10$\times$ larger Qwen2.5-VL-72B (50.5% vs. 47.3%).

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Recover, Discover, Plan: Learning Skills and Concepts from Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18328)
*Bowen Li,Mayank Mishra,Y. Isabel Liu,Stone Tao,Nishanth Kumar,Alexander G. Gray,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Sebastian Scherer,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReSYNC是一种让机器人通过失败恢复经验逐步学习抽象关系谓词，从而实现失败规避与可泛化规划的增量式双阶段学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法只能学习针对特定失败的反应式恢复行为，为每种失败模式训练独立策略效率低下。机器人需要从经验中提取抽象知识，以在未来规避同类失败，而非仅事后恢复。

Method: 提出ReSYNC框架，包含增量式双学习过程：1) 技能学习阶段：使用强化学习学习从已知失败中恢复的技能；2) 概念学习阶段：从恢复经验中发现新的关系谓词，并精炼抽象规划模型以解释和泛化恢复行为。

Result: 在四个模拟环境中的实验表明，ReSYNC通过持续扩展和精炼抽象库，能解决长期、未见过的问题，性能比强基线高出50%以上。此外，ReSYNC实现了从模拟到真实的迁移，能在真实世界执行非抓取操作技能，并通过抽象规划泛化到未见场景。

Conclusion: ReSYNC代表了机器人自主获取抽象、实现可扩展的、具有失败感知的物理世界规划的重要一步，显著提升了机器人的泛化与故障规避能力。

Abstract: Intelligent robots should not only recover from failures, but also acquire the abstract knowledge needed to avoid them in the future. While reinforcement learning (RL) can learn reactive recovery behaviors, training a separate policy for every distinct failure mode is highly inefficient. We introduce Recovery-Driven Synthesis of Relational Concepts (ReSYNC), the first approach that progressively discovers and refines state abstractions (relational predicates) from failure-recovery experience to support abstract planning. Unlike purely reactive methods, ReSYNC jointly learns skills and concepts through an incremental dual-learning process. In the skill-learning phase, the robot uses RL to learn to recover from failures seen in training tasks. In the concept-learning phase, the robot discovers new relational predicates and refines its abstract planning model to explain and generalize the learned recovery behaviors. This interaction enables ReSYNC to convert local recoveries seen during training into global failure avoidance at test time. Across four simulated domains, we show that ReSYNC's ability to continually expand and refine its abstraction library allows it to solve long-horizon, previously unseen problems, outperforming strong baselines by over 50%. Additionally, we demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of ReSYNC, where it performs real-world non-prehensile manipulation skills and generalizes to unseen scenarios through abstract planning. Overall, ReSYNC represents a significant step toward robots that autonomously acquire abstractions for scalable, failure-aware planning in the physical world.

</details>


### [87] [Guava: An Effective and Universal Harness for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18363)
*Haowen Liu,Xirui Li,Shaoxiong Yao,Peng Shi,Tianyi Zhou,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Language models trained on large-scale vision-language data have demonstrated strong potential for embodied agents. Harnessing models through embodied tools use offers a promising alternative to end-to-end vision-language-action systems by combining high-level reasoning with external modules for perception, planning, and control. However, it remains unclear what makes an effective harness for embodied manipulation, and to what extent such a harness can unlock embodied capabilities in a wide range of reasoning models. In this work, we present Guava, a harness framework for embodied tool use developed through systematic exploration of the design space of agent workflows, action spaces, and observation spaces. Our study identifies three key ingredients for effective embodied agents: iterative perception-reasoning-action loops, semantic action abstractions, and multimodal observations. To understand whether these design principles are universal even to small models, we develop an end-to-end training pipeline that distills embodied manipulation capabilities into a 4B open-source model using fewer than 2K trajectories collected entirely in simulation. Experimental results in both simulation and real-world environments show performance comparable to frontier proprietary models while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen objects, novel instructions, and long-horizon tasks. Results suggest that a well-designed harness can serve as a scalable, model-agnostic interface for embodied manipulation, enabling strong emergent embodied capabilities in compact open-source models with minimal training data.

</details>


### [88] [PAIWorld: A 3D-Consistent World Foundation Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18375)
*Yuhang Huang,Xuan Lv,Junyan Xu,Zhiyuan Yu,Jiazhao Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Wancheng Feng,Shilong Zou,Hewen Xiao,Ziqiao Zhou,Kaiyun Huang,Zhiyu Peng,Juzhan Xu,Hang Zhao,Chenyang Zhu,Renjiao Yi,Yifei Huang,Douhui Wu,Yan Zhang,Kexu Cheng,Chunhe Song,Yunzhi Xue,Xiuhong Zhang,Leitao Guo,Yunji Chen,Bin Wu,Haibin Yu,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAIWorld框架，旨在解决现有世界基础模型在机器人操作任务中缺乏多视角3D一致性的问题。该框架通过增强扩散变换器模型，引入几何感知的跨视角注意力、几何旋转位置嵌入和潜在3D-REPA等机制，在多个基准测试中达到了领先的多视角一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的世界基础模型主要在单视角下运行，缺乏机器人操作所需的多视角3D一致性。简单的多视角世界模型直接拼接视角标记，缺乏显式的几何推理，导致跨视角物体漂移、深度不一致和纹理错位。这些问题源于两个缺陷：缺少显式的跨视角通信机制和缺少3D几何先验。

Method: PAIWorld框架在扩散变换器基础上，通过三个核心组件来增强模型：1. 几何感知跨视角注意力块：建立跨视角的显式通信路径。2. 几何旋转位置嵌入：将相机射线方向和外部位姿编码到注意力机制中。3. 潜在3D-REPA：从冻结的3D基础模型中蒸馏3D感知特征，以确保3D一致性。

Result: 在机器人操作基准测试中，PAIWorld实现了最先进的多视角3D一致性。在WorldArena排行榜上排名第一，在AgiBot-Challenge2026排行榜上排名第二。该方法还支持下游应用，如基于模型的规划、世界动作模型和多视角策略后训练。

Conclusion: PAIWorld通过显式的跨视角几何通信和3D先验知识，有效解决了世界基础模型在多视角3D一致性上的不足，并在机器人操作任务中取得了领先性能，推动了其在相关下游任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: World foundation models (WFMs) are powerful simulators, yet they predominantly operate in a single-view setting and lack the multi-view 3D consistency required for robotic manipulation. While robotic systems rely on multiple cameras (egocentric, eye-to-hand, and wrist-mounted) for policy learning, current multi-view world models simply concatenate view tokens without explicit geometric reasoning. This causes cross-view object drift, depth inconsistency, and texture misalignment. We trace these failures to two deficiencies: the absence of an explicit inter-view communication mechanism and the lack of a 3D geometric prior. We argue that resolving both simultaneously is necessary and sufficient. To address this, we present PAIWorld, a framework that augments diffusion-transformer world models via three core components: (1) Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention blocks that establish an explicit pathway across views, (2) Geometric Rotary Position Embedding that encodes camera ray directions and extrinsic poses into the attention mechanism, and (3) Latent 3D-REPA, which distills 3D-aware features from frozen 3D foundation models to ensure 3D consistency. Built upon a DiT-based world foundation model, PAIWorld achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D consistency on robotic manipulation benchmarks, ranking 1st on the WorldArena leaderboard and 2nd on the AgiBot-Challenge2026 leaderboard, while enabling downstream applications such as model-based planning, world action models, and multi-view policy post-training.

</details>


### [89] [VEGA: Learning Navigation VLAs from In-the-Wild Egocentric Video with Geometric Trajectory Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18426)
*Gershom Seneviratne,Yohan Abeysinghe,Jianyu An,Vaibhav Shende,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了VEGA方法，利用无标签的自我中心导航视频训练导航视觉语言动作模型。通过从单目视频重建场景几何、采样导航目标并生成障碍物感知轨迹来训练流匹配VLA策略，并引入了VEGA-Bench评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的自我中心视频提供了可扩展的导航相关视觉观察来源，但这些视频无法直接用于策略学习，因为它们不包含基于显式导航目标、在机器人坐标系中的障碍物感知轨迹。VEGA旨在解决这一差距。

Method: 1. 从单目视频重建局部场景几何。
2. 采样导航目标（文本、图像或空间路点表示）。
3. 使用构建的几何生成障碍物感知轨迹。
4. 利用生成的轨迹分布训练流匹配视觉语言动作（VLA）导航策略。
5. 仅在训练阶段使用几何信息，将障碍物感知规划直接蒸馏到基于视觉的策略中。

Result: 在VEGA-Bench基准测试（包含25万场景和约500万导航目标）中，VEGA在目标进度方面具有竞争力，同时相比最强基线，碰撞减少33.0%，障碍物清除率提高17.9%。在真实世界试验中，成功率提升至少150.0%，碰撞减少至少66.7%，障碍物清除率提高至少60.0%。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的几何监督为训练障碍物感知的导航视觉语言动作模型提供了一种可扩展且有效的信号。代码和基准将在发表时发布。

Abstract: We introduce VEGA, an approach for training navigation VisionLanguage-Action (VLA) models from unlabeled egocentric navigation videos. Internet-scale egocentric videos provide a scalable source of navigation-relevant visual observations, capturing cluttered scenes, close-range obstacles, and natural human motion through real-world spaces. However, these videos are not directly usable for policy learning because they do not provide obstacle-aware trajectories conditioned on explicit navigation goals in the robot's coordinate frame. VEGA addresses this gap by reconstructing local scene geometry from monocular video, sampling navigation goals (represented as text, image, or spatial waypoints) and generating obstacle-aware trajectories using the constructed geometry. The resulting trajectory distribution is then used to train a flow-matching VLA navigation policy. By using geometry exclusively during training, VEGA distills obstacle-aware planning directly into a vision-based policy. Furthermore, we introduce VEGA-Bench, a benchmark containing 250k scenes and approximately 5 million navigation goals paired with scene geometry, designed to evaluate goal progress, collision avoidance, and obstacle clearance of VLAs. Our evaluation shows that VEGA achieves competitive goal progress while reducing collisions by 33.0% and improving obstacle clearance by 17.9% over the strongest baseline on VEGABench, while improving success by at least 150.0%, reducing collisions by at least 66.7%, and improving obstacle clearance by at least 60.0% in real-world trials. Ultimately, we demonstrate that video-derived geometric supervision provides a scalable and effective signal for training obstacle-aware navigation VLAs. The code and benchmark will be released at the time of publication.

</details>


### [90] [N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance Constraints to Solve Stochastic Orienteering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18514)
*Anas Saeed,Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了N(CO)²方法，将神经组合优化与机会约束相结合，用于解决随机定向旅行商问题（Stochastic Orienteering Problem），通过强化学习框架在不确定性环境下优化路径选择，无需手工设计启发式算法，实验结果表明该方法在多种SOP实例上表现良好，与最先进的混合整数线性规划方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）在确定性组合优化问题上已有广泛研究，但在随机组合优化问题上的研究较少。传统方法依赖手工设计的启发式规则，难以适应不确定环境。因此，需要一种能够自动学习启发式并处理随机性的新方法来解决这类问题。

Method: 提出N(CO)²（Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance cOnstraints）框架，整合强化学习（RL）方法，在不确定性环境下进行路径选择优化。该方法通过机会约束处理随机性，平衡探索与利用，实现自适应决策，无需手工设计启发式算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够很好地泛化到多种随机定向旅行商问题实例上，在性能上与最先进的混合整数线性规划（MILP）方法具有竞争力，同时减少了人工设计启发式的工作量。

Conclusion: 本文成功将神经组合优化扩展到随机组合优化领域，通过引入机会约束的强化学习框架，实现了在不确定环境下高效、自适应的决策。该方法为解决随机组合优化问题提供了一种有效的新途径，降低了对人工设计启发式的依赖。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) offers a promising alternative to traditional heuristic-based methods for solving complex graph optimization problems by proposing to learn heuristics through data. This class of problems frequently arises in automation, as it can be used to model a variety of applications. While NCO has been extensively studied for deterministic combinatorial optimization problems, there are only a few works that aim to solve stochastic combinatorial optimization problems. In this work, we present N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance cOnstraints to solve the Stochastic Orienteering Problem (SOP) without the use of hand-crafted heuristics. By integrating a reinforcement learning (RL) framework, the model optimizes path selection under uncertainty, effectively balancing exploration and exploitation. Empirical results demonstrate that our method generalizes well across diverse SOP instances, achieving competitive performance compared to the state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear program (MILP) for the task. The proposed approach reduces human effort in heuristic design while enabling adaptive and efficient decision-making in uncertain environments.

</details>


### [91] [Task Allocation and Motion Planning in Dynamic, Cluttered Environments via CBBA and Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18516)
*Matthew D. Osburn,Cameron K. Peterson,John L. Salmon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合图凸集(GCS)和基于共识的束算法(CBBA)的方法，用于解决杂乱动态环境中多智能体任务分配和轨迹规划的耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱、动态的环境中进行多智能体任务规划时，需要同时完成任务分配和轨迹规划。当任务是动态的（如会合目标）时，分配决策不仅取决于哪个智能体最适合任务，还取决于何时何地能够到达任务点。传统的任务分配和轨迹规划方法通常是分离的，无法有效处理这种耦合关系。

Method: 结合图凸集(GCS)和基于共识的束算法(CBBA)。GCS使用时间扩展的（3D+时间）配置空间在动态环境中寻找最优轨迹。CBBA协调跨智能体的任务分配，实现在移动环境中的知情决策。将分配和规划连接起来，使智能体能够在3D+时间配置空间中避免碰撞，并提供准确的任务完成时间估计。

Result: 通过模拟杂乱环境中的静态和动态任务，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效解决杂乱动态环境中多智能体任务分配和轨迹规划的耦合问题，实现安全、时间高效的轨迹规划和任务分配。

Abstract: Multi-agent task planning in cluttered, dynamic environments requires assigning tasks to agents while simultaneously determining safe, time-efficient trajectories through the environment. When tasks are dynamic, such as rendezvous objectives, allocation decisions depend not only on which agent is best suited for a task, but also on when and where that task can be reached. This paper presents a solution to this problem, which combines Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) for trajectory optimization with the Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA) for distributed task allocation. In our approach, GCS finds optimal trajectories through dynamic environments using a time-extended (3D+time) configuration space. At the same time, CBBA coordinates task assignments across agents, enabling informed decision-making in a moving environment. We then connect allocation and planning to allow the agents to avoid collisions in the 3D+time configuration space and provide accurate time estimates for task completion. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in simulated cluttered environments with static and dynamic tasks.

</details>


### [92] [As You Wish: Mission Planning with Formal Verification using LLMs in Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18519)
*Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对农业机器人任务规划系统中自然语言描述的固有模糊性问题，本文通过引入基于线性时序逻辑的多反馈循环和验证机制，提升了系统的规范符合性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统部署需要高技能操作，先前引入基于大语言模型的任务规划系统虽性能出色，但其依赖的自然语言输入存在固有模糊性，可能导致任务执行偏离用户意图，需要解决这一可靠性问题。

Method: 1. 在规划架构中引入多个反馈循环；2. 利用线性时序逻辑来形式化用户规范；3. 采用两个不同的商业大语言模型分别负责规范制定和验证子任务，以减轻潜在偏差。

Result: 通过大量实验，评估了将任务验证集成到全自主流程中的优势与局限性，特别分析了大语言模型生成有效LTL公式的能力，并展示了所提实施方案如何应对和解决这些挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究通过集成基于LTL的形式化验证和多LLM反馈机制，有效提升了自然语言驱动任务规划系统的规范符合性和鲁棒性，解决了自然语言模糊性带来的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Though robotic systems are now being commercialized and deployed in various industries, many of these systems are highly specialized and often require an advanced skill set to operate and ensure they perform as instructed. To mitigate this problem, we recently introduced a mission planner leveraging LLMs to synthesize mission plans in precision agriculture based on mission descriptions provided in natural language. While the system demonstrates impressive performance, it also suffers from the inherent ambiguities of natural language. In this paper, we extend our system to address this issue by introducing multiple feedback loops in the planning architecture that leverage linear temporal logic (LTL) to ensure the mission planning system meets the specifications formulated by the user while still using natural language. To mitigate potential bias, this is achieved by using two different commercial LLMs in charge of the specification and verification subtasks. Through extensive experiments, we highlight the strengths and limitations of integrating mission verification into a fully autonomous pipeline, particularly regarding an LLM's ability to generate valuable LTL formulas, and show how our proposed implementation addresses and solves these challenges.

</details>


### [93] [DREAM-Chunk: Reactive Action Chunking with Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18589)
*Wenxi Chen,Kaidi Zhang,Chi Lin,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yu She,Yuejiang Liu,Raymond A. Yeh,Shaoshuai Mou,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM-Chunk，一种测试时缩放方法，通过引入轻量级潜在世界模型来增强基于动作分块的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的鲁棒性，无需对策略本身进行微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块是VLA模型中的常用接口，但其开环执行在面对随机动力学、硬件执行误差和部分可观测性时往往表现脆弱，导致策略在实际部署中鲁棒性不足。

Method: 在测试时，DREAM-Chunk对候选动作分块进行采样，并利用潜在世界模型推出每个分块的潜在未来状态，然后选择预测状态与实际观测推出序列最匹配的分块中的动作来执行。该方法通过增加测试时计算来覆盖多种可能的随机未来，从而提升长时程动作执行过程中的反应性。

Result: 在Kinetix基准测试中，DREAM-Chunk在增加动作噪声的情况下显著提升了策略鲁棒性，并且受益于更大的候选样本量，尤其当演示数据包含纠正行为时效果更佳。进一步在两个机器人平台、两个VLA策略及四个操作任务上的实验验证了该方法在多种随机性来源下的有效性。

Conclusion: DREAM-Chunk是一种有效的测试时缩放方法，能够显著提升动作分块策略在随机动态环境下的鲁棒性，且无需对原始策略进行额外训练，为VLA模型的实际部署提供了可行的增强方案。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common interface for vision-language-action (VLA) models, enabling low-frequency policy inference to drive high-frequency robot execution. However, once an action chunk is committed, its open-loop execution can be brittle under stochastic dynamics, hardware execution errors, and partial observability. We propose DREAM-Chunk, a test-time scaling method that augments chunking-based policies with a lightweight latent world model, without requiring additional policy fine-tuning. At test time, DREAM-Chunk samples multiple candidate action chunks, rolls out their predicted latent futures, and selects actions from the chunk whose predicted state best matches the observed rollout. In this way, DREAM-Chunk uses additional test-time computation to cover multiple plausible stochastic futures and improve reactivity during long-horizon chunk execution. On the Kinetix benchmark, DREAM-Chunk improves robustness under increasing action noise and benefits from larger candidate sample sizes, especially when demonstrations contain corrective behaviors. We further validate DREAM-Chunk on four manipulation tasks across two robot platforms and two VLA policies under various sources of stochasticity. Across simulation and hardware experiments, DREAM-Chunk improves the robustness of action-chunking policies in stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [94] [Benchmarking Action Spaces in Reinforcement Learning for Vision-based Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18594)
*Seyed Alireza Azimi,Homayoon Farrahi,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过评估位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量和关节速度四种动作空间表示在物体抓取和推物体两个视觉操作任务上的表现，发现关节速度动作空间在运动平滑性和最终任务性能上最优，为强化学习实践者提供了动作空间选择的实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界强化学习中，动作空间的选择对运动平滑性、安全性和整体任务性能有关键影响。本研究旨在系统评估不同动作空间表示（特别是视觉操作任务中的）对 sim-to-real 性能的影响，以提供选择依据。

Method: 在模拟环境中训练策略，并通过 sim-to-real 迁移部署到现实世界。在两个视觉操作任务（物体抓取和推物体）上，比较了四种动作空间表示：位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量和关节速度。

Result: 动作空间的表示确实显著影响 sim-to-real 的迁移性能。具体而言，关节速度动作空间在视觉抓取和推物体任务中，无论是运动平滑性还是最终任务完成度，都表现最佳。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的视觉操作任务，关节速度动作空间是最优选择。该研究为强化学习实践者在模拟和现实世界实验中选择合适的动作空间提供了实用的指导原则。

Abstract: In real-world reinforcement learning (RL), the choice of action space can play a key role in shaping motion smoothness, safety, and overall task performance. In this study, we evaluate pose increment, pose velocity, joint position increment, and joint velocity across two vision-based manipulation tasks: object picking and pushing. We train policies in simulation and deploy them to the real world using sim-to-real transfer. We find that action-space representation indeed significantly affects sim-to-real performance. In particular, we find that the joint velocity action space is best for the vision-based picking and pushing tasks in terms of smoothness and final task performance. We also provide practical guidance for RL practitioners in choosing action spaces for both simulation and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [95] [Admittance-Based Surface Alignment for Human-in-the-Loop Robotic Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18601)
*Antara Banerjee,Colin Acton,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对工业高价值部件表面精密视觉检测的需求，本文提出了一种基于导纳控制的实时、闭环机器人定向控制管线，它将操作员输入与感知驱动的表面几何对齐统一起来，实现了稳定的法线跟踪，最终平均定向误差为0.4°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空航天、半导体和医疗制造等领域，精密视觉检测对质量保证至关重要，未检测到的表面缺陷会导致报废、返工和现场故障。机器人视觉检测需要末端执行器与局部表面几何形状精确对齐，且存在感知噪声和表面不规则性。在实际工业场景中，通常通过遥操作或共享自主权让人机操作者参与实时调整，这使得纯粹的离线运动规划不足。因此，需要一种能够在人类和感知不确定性并存下实现反应式、柔顺行为的控制架构。

Method: 本文提出了一种新颖的、实时、闭环的机器人定向控制管线。该方法采用一种基于导纳的控制框架，将操作员输入与感知驱动的表面对齐统一起来。具体设计是将末端执行器建模为在粘性介质中运动的虚拟球体，由此产生的、物理可解释的质量-阻尼系统，能够根据定向误差和操作员指令生成同步、柔顺的运动。

Result: 在一个6自由度机械手上对该框架进行了实验验证，结果表明该方法能够实现稳定的法线跟踪，最终平均定向误差为0.4°。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于导纳的控制框架，成功解决了精密视觉检测中机器人末端执行器在不确定性和人为干预下的精确、实时定向对齐问题。该方法通过将操作员指令融入感知反馈的柔顺控制回路，为高价值部件的自动化质量检测提供了有效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Precision visual inspection underpins quality assurance across aerospace, semiconductor, and medical manufacturing, where undetected surface anomalies on high-value parts translate directly into scrap, rework, and field failures. Robotic visual inspection requires precise alignment between the end-effector and local surface geometry in the presence of perception noise and surface irregularities. In industrial settings, a human operator is often kept in the loop via teleoperation or shared autonomy, introducing real-time adjustments that render purely offline motion planning inadequate. This motivates control architectures capable of reactive, compliant behavior under combined human and perceptual uncertainty. This paper presents a novel real-time, closed-loop robotic orientation control pipeline for precision visual inspection, with an admittance-based framework that unifies operator input and perception-driven surface alignment. We design the end-effector as a virtual sphere moving through a viscous medium, such that the resulting physically interpretable mass--damper system generates synchronized, compliant motion from orientation error and operator commands. We validate the framework on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrating stable normal-tracking and a final mean orientation error of 0.4°.

</details>


### [96] [SC3-Eval: Evaluating Robot Foundation Models via Self-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18610)
*Wei-Cheng Tseng,Gashon Hussein,Yuzhu Dong,Allen Z. Ren,Lucy X. Shi,XuDong Wang,Sergey Levine,Zhaoshuo Li,Jinwei Gu,Florian Shkurti,Ming-Yu Liu,Quan Vuong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SC3-Eval是一种自洽的视频生成方案，通过将预训练视频基础模型适应为准确的策略评估器，利用前向-逆向动力学一致性、跨视图一致性和测试时一致性三种互补的一致性，解决视频世界模型在评估通用机器人操作策略时面临的误差累积、多视角不一致和泛化问题。该方法在现实世界策略评估中展现出优异的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界中评估通用机器人操作策略成本高昂、速度缓慢且难以扩展。基于动作条件视频世界模型的模拟评估是一个可扩展的替代方案，但面临自回归展开中的累积误差、多视角观察的互一致性要求以及评估器需泛化到训练分布外策略等挑战。

Method: SC3-Eval采用三种互补的一致性训练预训练视频模型：1）前向-逆向动力学一致性：联合训练模型从动作预测帧和从帧恢复动作，将生成的展开锚定到物理合理的动作流形；2）跨视图一致性：训练模型从其他视角修复每个摄像头视角，保持多视角观察的长期一致性；3）测试时一致性：在推理时重用逆向动力学模型作为每个动作块的不确定性信号，终止生成帧偏离请求动作的展开。

Result: 在七个现实世界的视觉-语言-动作策略上，SC3-Eval达到了0.929的闭环皮尔逊相关系数和0.119的MMRV，优于三个基于视频模型的基线方法，并且能够泛化到新任务。此外，其展开过程能重现策略在现实世界中出现的失败模式。

Conclusion: SC3-Eval通过强制实施三种一致性，成功将预训练视频模型转化为有效的策略评估器。它不仅在评估准确性上超越现有基线，而且能够支持细粒度的诊断比较（如复现失败模式），而不仅仅是提供聚合排名，为通用机器人操作策略的评估提供了一个可扩展且可靠的模拟方案。

Abstract: Evaluating generalist robot manipulation policies in the real world is expensive, slow, and difficult to scale. Action-conditioned video world models offer a scalable alternative by simulating policy rollouts. Autoregressive rollouts accumulate compounding errors, observations across multiple camera views must remain mutually consistent, and the evaluator must generalize to policies whose behaviors lie outside the training distribution. We address these challenges with SC3-Eval, a self-consistent video generation recipe that adapts a pre-trained video foundation model into an accurate policy evaluator by enforcing three complementary forms of consistency. First, forward-inverse dynamics consistency jointly trains the model to predict frames from actions and to recover actions from frames, anchoring generated rollouts to a physically plausible action manifold and counteracting the drift a forward-only model cannot penalize. Second, cross-view consistency trains the model to inpaint each camera view from the other, keeping the multi-camera observation coherent over long rollouts without any explicit memory mechanism. Third, test-time consistency reuses the inverse dynamics mode at inference as a per-action-chunk uncertainty signal that terminates rollouts whose generated frames drift away from the requested actions. We also demonstrate SC3-Eval rollouts reproduce the failure modes that policies exhibit in real-world rollouts, supporting fine-grained diagnostic comparison rather than aggregate ranking alone. Across seven real-world vision-language-action policies, SC3-Eval attains a closed-loop Pearson correlation of $0.929$ and MMRV of $0.119$, outperforming three strong prior video-model-based baselines, and generalizes to new tasks.

</details>


### [97] [SRL: Combining SLIP Model and Reinforcement Learning for Agile Robotic Jumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18625)
*Xiaowen Hu,Linqi Ye,Yudi Zhu,Chenyue Shao,Rankun Li,Qingdu Li,Yan Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为“弹簧负载强化学习”（SRL）的混合控制框架，该框架整合了基于SLIP模型的前馈控制信号与强化学习驱动的实时反馈，用于优化机器人跳跃。与基线方法相比，SRL显著减少了训练时间并提高了跳跃的稳定性和跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人跳跃在搜索和救援等应用中至关重要。现有的弹簧负载倒立摆（SLIP）模型虽然能编码生物合理的跳跃运动，但在不规则地形上因理想化假设而性能下降。另一方面，强化学习（RL）虽然能适应复杂环境，但通常需要大量无引导探索的数据。因此，研究的动机是利用SLIP的物理基础和RL的自适应能力的互补优势，创建一个混合框架来克服各自的局限性。

Method: 本文提出了SRL框架，其核心是将SLIP模型提供的基于物理的前馈控制信号与RL生成的实时反馈信号相结合。这种整合使得系统能够进行连续优化，从而在保持SLIP模型物理合理性的同时，通过RL实现对环境变化的快速自适应。

Result: 实验结果表明，SRL框架实现了更稳定的跳跃，同时所需训练时间远少于基线方法。具体性能指标为：平均位置跟踪误差低于0.1米，速度跟踪误差保持在目标值的±3%以内。此外，通过在双足和四足机器人上的地面及楼梯跳跃仿真，以及sim-to-sim和sim-to-real的迁移验证，证明了SRL对各种任务需求和环境复杂性具有强大的鲁棒适应性。

Conclusion: SRL框架成功结合了SLIP模型和强化学习的优点，为机器人跳跃任务提供了一种高效、稳定且自适应的控制方案。其在多种仿真及向真实环境迁移中展现出的鲁棒性，凸显了该框架在实际应用（如搜索救援和物流）中部署的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Robotic jumping is pivotal in applications such as search and rescue and logistics, where crossing obstacles and enhancing mobility efficiency are critical. The Spring-Loaded Inverted Pendulum (SLIP) model leverages simplified spring-mass dynamics that naturally encode biologically plausible hopping motions, yet its performance degrades on irregular terrain due to idealized assumptions regarding contact and joint dynamics. Meanwhile, Reinforcement Learning (RL) can adapt to diverse and complex environments but often requires extensive data from unguided exploration. The complementary strengths of SLIP's physically grounded baseline and RL's adaptive capabilities motivate a hybrid framework that overcomes these individual limitations. We therefore propose Spring-loaded Reinforcement Learning (SRL), which integrates SLIP-based feedforward control signals with RL-driven real-time feedback, enabling continuous optimization of robotic jumping. Experimental results demonstrate that SRL can achieve more stable jumps with much less training time than the baseline method, maintaining an average position tracking error below 0.1 m and velocity tracking errors within +/-3% of the target values. Through bipedal and quadrupedal simulations of ground and stair jumping, as well as sim-to-sim and sim-to-real validations, SRL exhibits robust adaptability to various task requirements and environmental complexities, underscoring its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [98] [Self-Supervised Mask-Aware Transformers for Fault-Tolerant FBG Force Sensing in Minimally Invasive Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18628)
*Peibo Sun,Shiyuan Dong,Shucheng Ye,Jianrong Cai,Yushan Liu,Hongen Liao,Tianqi Huang,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文针对微创手术机器人中光纤布拉格光栅传感器因非线性耦合和通道中断导致的力估计失效问题，提出了一种统一的自监督掩码感知Transformer模型。该模型通过掩码通道重构进行预训练，能在单次前向传播中同时实现鲁棒的力估计和不确定性量化，在传感器故障情况下性能显著优于依赖大量独立模型的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术机器人中使用的导管级多通道FBG传感器面临两个核心挑战：复杂变形时固有的非线性交叉轴耦合，以及受限工作空间内光纤断裂导致的间歇性通道中断。这两个问题共同严重降低了力估计的精度。现有的容错方法依赖于组合模型库，其计算复杂度随通道数指数增长，且每种故障模式都需要昂贵的单独校准，难以实际应用。因此，亟需一种能统一处理多种动态传感器故障、实现优雅降级且校准开销低的方法。

Method: 作者提出了一种统一的自监督掩码感知Transformer模型。其核心方法包括：1) 编码器通过在未标记数据流上进行掩码通道重建进行自监督预训练，学习通道间的相关性和鲁棒特征；2) 针对力回归任务，使用一个平衡的“干净与损坏视图”目标进行微调，并结合动态损坏课程以增强模型对多种故障模式的泛化能力；3) 并行引入一个不确定性预测头，通过异方差高斯负对数似然损失训练，在单次前向传播中输出每个轴向力的预测值及其置信度，避免了模型集成所需的多次前向传播。

Result: 在包含8个通道的导管级FBG数据集上，该单一统一模型在正常（无故障）情况下的力估计均方根误差（RMSE）为0.0066 N。在面临严重故障（如4个通道失效）时，模型能优雅降级，RMSE仅为0.0126 N。这一性能显著优于一个包含255个独立神经网络（对应不同通道故障模式）的综合模型库，后者的RMSE在4通道失效时为0.0154 N。这表明所提方法无需针对特定故障模式进行校准，即可实现更优且稳定的力估计。

Conclusion: 本文成功提出了一种针对FBG传感器力估计的统一容错框架。该方法通过自监督预训练和动态损坏课程，有效学习了传感器通道的鲁棒表示，解决了非线性耦合与通道中断的复合难题。其核心创新在于将故障模式隐式编码到单一模型的结构中，并通过并行不确定性量化提供可靠的置信度评估，同时避免了传统模型库的组合爆炸问题和高额校准成本。实验结果证明了该方法在提升手术机器人触觉感知可靠性方面的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: In minimally invasive surgical robotics, catheter-scale Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) sensors are promising due to their ability to estimate multi-dimensional forces by multiplexing several optical channels. However, deploying these compact multi-channel sensors introduces two critical engineering challenges: inherent nonlinear cross-axis coupling during complex deformations, and intermittent channel dropouts caused by fiber fractures in constrained workspaces. These compounding issues severely degrade force estimation. Existing fault-tolerant approaches rely on combinatorial model banks, which scale exponentially with the channel count and demand prohibitively expensive per-pattern calibration. In this paper, we propose a unified, self-supervised mask-aware Transformer that explicitly models channel availability to enable graceful degradation under diverse and dynamic sensor failures. The encoder is pretrained via masked-channel reconstruction on unlabeled data streams and fine-tuned for force regression using a balanced clean-and-corrupted-view objective alongside a dynamic corruption curriculum. Furthermore, a parallel uncertainty head, trained via heteroscedastic Gaussian negative log-likelihood, predicts per-axis confidence in a single forward pass, circumventing the overhead of multi-pass ensembles. Evaluated on a catheter-scale 8-channel FBG dataset, our single unified model achieves a nominal Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 0.0066~N and degrades gracefully to 0.0126~N under severe 4-channel failures. This significantly outperforms a comprehensive model bank of 255 per-pattern neural networks (0.0154~N at 4-channel loss) while eliminating pattern-specific calibration.

</details>


### [99] [DNN Koopman-Based Deviation Compensation for UGV Path Tracking Control on Coupled Slope and Potholed Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18630)
*Jian Zhao,Wenbo Zhou,Zhicheng Chen,Bing Zhu,Jiayi Han,Dongjian Song,Yinju Lin,Peixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度神经网络（DNN）Koopman理论的无人地面车辆（UGV）路径跟踪控制策略，通过结合拉盖尔模型预测控制（LMPC）与事件触发并行协作（EPC）补偿机制，有效提升了车辆在复杂越野地形下的跟踪精度与稳定性，实验表明性能提升超过11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野场景下的UGV面临复杂地形干扰（如耦合坡道、坑洼路面），这严重降低了路径跟踪性能，因此需要开发一种鲁棒的控制策略来抑制干扰、提高跟踪精度与稳定性。

Method: 首先，设计具有解耦误差项的自适应遗忘递归最小二乘法估计轮胎侧偏刚度；其次，结合拉盖尔函数设计LMPC路径跟踪控制策略以降低计算资源消耗；然后，集成Koopman算子理论与DNN提出DK路径偏差补偿方法；进而，基于补偿激活标准与可信度验证建立耦合LMPC与DK的EPC补偿机制；最后，搭建硬件在环（HiL）实验平台进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的UGV路径跟踪策略在多种工况下将跟踪性能提升了超过11.5%，有效改善了在坑洼路面等复杂干扰下的路径跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的DNN Koopman偏差补偿策略结合LMPC与EPC机制，能够在保证计算效率与系统可行性的前提下，显著提升UGV在复杂越野地形下的路径跟踪精度与鲁棒性，为相关控制系统设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) operating in off-road scenarios are confronted with complex terrain disturbances that can substantially degrade path tracking performance. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a deep neural network (DNN) Koopman-based deviation compensation strategy for UGV path tracking control. Firstly, based on the vehicle dynamic function on coupled slope, an adaptive forgetting recursive least squares method with decoupled error terms is designed to estimate tire cornering stiffness. On this basis, a Laguerre model predictive control (LMPC) path tracking control strategy is designed by incorporating Laguerre functions, which can reduce computational resource usage while maintaining reliable tracking performance across different coupled slope scenarios. Then, by integrating Koopman operator theory with DNN, a DNN Koopman (DK) path deviation compensation method is proposed, which significantly improves the path tracking accuracy of UGV under potholed road disturbances. Furthermore, an event-triggered parallel cooperative (EPC) compensation mechanism that couples LMPC with DK is established based on compensation activation criteria and credibility verification. This mechanism improves path tracking accuracy on potholed road while ensuring the feasibility of overall steering command and stability of vehicle after DK compensation. Finally, a hardware-in-the-loop (HiL) experimental platform is constructed for validation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed UGV path tracking strategy improves tracking performance by more than 11.5% across multiple operating conditions.

</details>


### [100] [ROBOSHACKLES: A Safety Dataset for Human-Injury Prevention in Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18632)
*Zhuowen Yin,Chongyang Liu,Wenzhang Yang,Renjue Li,Yinxing Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于评估具身基础模型防止人类受伤安全性的安全关键数据构建管道，并基于此构建了ROBOSHACKLES数据集。评估显示所有被测模型在安全场景中100%生成不安全动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型的安全对齐，特别是防止人类受伤方面，尚未充分探索，主要是因为无法安全或伦理地收集机器人伤害人类或造成危险家庭情况的真实数据。

Method: 从真实DROID观测出发，通过场景理解、危险感知图像编辑、时间提示生成和单次滚动合成构建数据。使用该管道构建了ROBOSHACKLES数据集，包含10,000个视频片段，涵盖两种直接伤害和四种间接伤害类别。

Result: 使用自动指标评估任务完成度和视觉质量，并在基于拒绝的安全标准下评估了六个代表性模型。结果显示所有被评估模型在测试的安全关键场景中都产生了不安全动作，不安全动作生成率达到100%。

Conclusion: ROBOSHACKLES可作为拒绝学习和危险预期的可扩展基准和训练资源，用于机器人动作执行前的安全评估。

Abstract: Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) integrate multimodal understanding, future-state reasoning, and executable robot actions. Yet their safety alignment for human-injury prevention remains underexplored, primarily because real-world data of robots harming humans or creating hazardous household situations cannot be safely or ethically collected. To address this challenge, we propose a safety-critical data construction pipeline for human-injury prevention in EFMs.Starting from real DROID observations, our construction pipeline proceeds through scene understanding, hazard-aware image editing, temporal prompt generation, and single-pass rollout synthesis. The temporal prompts specify the expected scene evolution, while Wan2.7 synthesizes realistic robotic rollouts from the edited hazardous states in a single pass. Using this pipeline, we construct ROBOSHACKLES, a 10,000-clip robotic video dataset derived from real DROID observations, spanning two direct-harm and four indirect-harm categories. To ensure dataset quality, we assess task completion and visual quality with automatic metrics, and evaluate six representative EFMs under a refusal-based safety criterion. Results show that all evaluated models produce unsafe actions in the tested safety-critical scenarios, yielding a 100% unsafe action generation rate. ROBOSHACKLES serves as a scalable benchmark and training resource for refusal learning and hazard anticipation before robot action execution.The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/YZW00/RoboShackles.

</details>


### [101] [EffiNav: Fusing Depth and Vision-Language for Efficient Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18634)
*Zecheng Yin,Benedict Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiNav系统，旨在提高目标物体导航（ObjNav）任务的效率与成功率。通过模拟和真实世界实验验证，该系统在标准评估指标上匹配或超越现有基线，并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中进行目标物体导航时，现有基于训练的模型存在泛化问题，而非训练框架则效率较低。这些问题可能导致过度探索已访问区域或冗余往返运动，因此需要一种更高效、稳健的导航方法。

Method: EffiNav系统通过智能决策探索顺序来提高导航效率。该方法在Habitat Matterport 3D（HM3D）和开放词汇目标导航（OVON）基准上进行评估，并在物理机器人上验证。此外，通过大规模模拟分析失败案例，并将方法扩展到记忆增强的目标导航任务（基于GOAT-BENCH数据集），以展示其适应性。

Result: 实验结果显示，EffiNav在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）两个标准指标上匹配或超越近期基线方法。该框架在效率、鲁棒性和实际应用性方面表现突出，且更平衡、可泛化。

Conclusion: EffiNav系统有效解决了目标物体导航中的效率和泛化问题，不仅在模拟环境中表现优异，还能扩展至真实世界任务和记忆增强场景，具有较高的实用价值和适应性。

Abstract: To locate a target object while exploring the unknown environment is a fundamental capability for autonomous agents, with applications ranging from search-and-rescue to field robots. A simplified version of such task is Object Goal Navigation (ObjNav). In ObjNav, successful arrival at the target object provides a basic measure of performance; however, the efficiency of the navigation trajectory is equally important, as it indicates how intelligently the agent explores and how much time remains for subsequent tasks. In unknown environments, the key to efficient navigation lies in deciding where to explore next. While many prior works aim to address this core challenge and achieved promising performance in certain settings, recent training-based models and non-training frameworks still suffer from generalization and efficiency issues respectively, which in the worst cases can lead to excessive exploration of already-visited areas or redundant back-and-forth motion. We evaluate EffiNav on two widely used simulation benchmarks Habitat Matterport 3D (HM3D) and Open-Vocabulary Object goal Navigation (OVON), and further validate its effectiveness on physical robots in real-world settings. We conduct failure analysis on massive simulation episodes. With minimal modification, we also extend EffiNav to a memory-augmented ObjNav task on the GOAT-BENCH dataset, demonstrating its adaptability beyond standard ObjNav settings. Across two standard metrics--Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), EffiNav matches or outperforms recent baselines, reflecting its efficiency, robustness, and practical applicability. Recognizing the different emphases of the two datasets, the performances reveals this framework is more balanced and generalizable for efficient ObjNav.

</details>


### [102] [A Scalable Embodied Intelligence Platform for Seamless Real-to-Sim-to-Real Transfer of Household Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18646)
*Kui Yang,Xianlei Long,Haoxuan Li,Yan Ding,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了名为BestMan的可扩展、无缝衔接的真实-仿真-真实平台，通过自动化场景生成、仿真引导的策略学习架构和硬件无关的统一中间件，解决了家庭移动操作中高保真仿真重建、策略评估及sim-to-real迁移的挑战，并通过实验验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作在具身智能机器人中至关重要，但在非结构化家庭环境中实现鲁棒、通用的操作面临三大挑战：1）重建高保真仿真场景成本高昂；2）在仿真中进行系统性策略评估复杂；3）真实世界部署时存在不兼容问题。这些挑战阻碍了有效的策略开发、集成与部署。

Method: 1. 设计了自动场景生成（ASG）模块，能从真实观察数据重建逼真的仿真环境。2. 提出了一种仿真引导的任务形式化与技能学习架构，支持混合技能策略的灵活集成和大规模评估。3. 开发了硬件无关且统一的中间件（HUM），确保在异构移动操作器上实现无缝且兼容的sim-to-real转移。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的BestMan平台在建立移动操作领域的标准化基准和推动有前景的研究方面表现优越，成功实现了有效策略的开发、集成与部署。

Conclusion: BestMan平台成功克服了真实-仿真-真实循环中的关键挑战，为家庭移动操作提供了可扩展、无缝的解决方案，促进了策略的有效开发、集成与部署，并推动了该领域的标准化研究与进步。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a fundamental capability in embodied intelligence robotics. The growing demand for robust and generalizable manipulation in unstructured household environments has driven rapid progress in embodied intelligence platforms. However, achieving a seamless transfer across the real-to-sim-to-real cycle faces three key challenges, including costly high-fidelity simulation scenes reconstruction, the complexity of systematic strategy evaluation in simulation, and incompatible real-world deployments. To address these challenges, we develop BestMan, a scalable and seamless real-to-sim-to-real platform that bridges the gap between the simulation and the real world, enabling effective strategy development, integration, and deployment for household mobile manipulation. Specifically, we design a novel Automated Scene Generation (ASG) module to reconstruct realistic simulations from real observations. Then, we propose a simulation-guided task formalization and skill learning architecture that supports the flexible integration and large-scale evaluations of hybrid skill strategies in simulation. Finally, to enhance the real-world scalability, we develop a Hardware-agnostic and Unified Middleware (HUM) to ensure seamless and compatible sim-to-real transfer across heterogeneous mobile manipulators for real deployments. Experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed platform in establishing standardized benchmarks and facilitating promising research in the field of mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [103] [High-Degree-of-Freedom Lightweight Bioinspired Leg for Enhanced Mobility in Small Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18680)
*Haoqi Han,Yifei Yu,Jiaming Zhang,Xinru Cui,Linxi Feng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文受昆虫运动启发，提出一种四自由度微型并联腿部机构，通过并联架构和同心设计实现了高灵活性与紧凑性，在微型仿生机器人中展现应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的空间约束下，通过增加腿部机构的自由度（DoF）来增强微型机器人的运动能力，仍然是该领域面临的重大挑战。

Method: 设计采用两个球面五杆机构在并联四杆配置中实现空间运动；运用同心设计策略简化腿部运动学的解析解；采用并联系统架构，将所有执行器置于主体上，以降低运动部件的等效惯性。

Result: 研制的单腿系统总质量仅18.9克，末端执行器输出力约为0.5牛，工作空间超过22255立方毫米。实验结果表明该机构具有优异的运动灵活性。

Conclusion: 所提出的四自由度微型并联腿部机构设计成功，在微型化和性能上取得了良好平衡，为下一代微型仿生机器人的发展提供了重要基础。

Abstract: In microrobotics, enhancing locomotion capabilities by increasing the degrees of freedom (DoF) of leg mechanisms under severe spatial constraints remains a significant challenge. Inspired by insect locomotion, this paper presents a novel micro-scale parallel leg mechanism with four degrees of freedom, and systematically analyzes its mechanical design, electrical system, and kinematics. The design incorporates two spherical five-bar linkages to achieve spatial motion within a parallel four-bar configuration. Furthermore, a concentric design strategy is employed to simplify the analytical solution of the leg kinematics. Due to the parallel system architecture, all actuators are located on the main body, substantially reducing the equivalent inertia of moving parts compared to traditional high-DOF leg structures. The total mass of the system is only 18.9 g, with an end-effector output force of approximately 0.5 N and a workspace exceeding 22255 mm3. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed single-leg mechanism achieves excellent motion flexibility, highlighting its potential for micro bio-inspired robotics.

</details>


### [104] [Leveraging Energy Features for Surface Classification with Deep Learning: A Comparative Analysis Across Three Independent Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18698)
*Alexander Belyaev,Oleg Kushnarev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于能量的特征在移动机器人表面分类中的可行性。通过比较多种深度学习架构，发现仅使用能量特征可达到85-90%的准确率，而与惯性数据结合使用时，准确率可提升至96-99%，并带来1-2%的稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于能量的方法在移动机器人表面分类中仍相对较少被研究，尽管其在受限环境中已显示出有前景的结果。本研究旨在评估能量衍生特征作为独立分类模式或与惯性数据结合使用的可行性。

Method: 研究在三个公开数据集上进行了全面评估，比较了循环神经网络、卷积神经网络、仅编码器转换器和Mamba状态空间模型等多种现代深度学习架构的性能。研究采用了自动超参数调优和输入序列长度优化。

Result: 在所有评估数据集上，模型都实现了比先前报道更高的准确率，其中卷积神经网络表现最佳。仅依赖能量特征时，分类准确率在85-90%范围内，比结合惯性特征时（96-99%）低约5-10%。将能量特征添加到惯性数据中，平均准确率稳定提高了1-2%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，仅依赖能量特征的分类器具有足够的准确性，可以独立部署；同时，将能量特征与其他传感模式（如惯性数据）结合使用时，也能提供一致的性能提升。

Abstract: The energy-based method remains a comparatively underexamined approach for surface classification in mobile robotics, despite promising results in constrained environments. This study evaluated the viability of using energy-derived features as either a standalone classification modality or as supplementary input to inertial data. A comprehensive evaluation was conducted across three publicly available datasets, comparing the performance of modern deep learning architectures including recurrent neural networks, convolutional neural networks, encoder-only transformers, and Mamba state-space models, under automated hyperparameter tuning and input sequence length optimization. The models achieved higher accuracy than previously reported values on all evaluated datasets, with the convolutional neural network yielding the highest overall performance. When relying exclusively on energy-based features, the models attained classification accuracies in the range of 85-90%, approximately 5-10% lower than those achieved when combined with inertial features (96-99%). Augmenting inertial data with energy features resulted in a consistent mean accuracy improvement of 1-2%. These findings indicate that classifiers relying solely on energy features offer sufficient accuracy for standalone deployment, while also providing a consistent gain when used in combination with other sensing modalities.

</details>


### [105] [Selective Unit-Cell Actuation in Lattice Structures for Distributed Morphology in Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18704)
*Trevor Exley,Altair Coutinho,Lucia Beccai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种集成致动器的软晶格单胞，实现分布式致动和可编程变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软机器人的致动通常是在设备或模块层面引入，本研究旨在将致动器与晶格的协同设计推进到单胞尺度，以实现更精细的分布式控制和形态变化。

Method: 提出了一种嵌入式气动单胞，将弯曲支撑晶格几何结构与双向波纹管致动器集成在单个整体元件中。通过阵列化该单胞，构建了一个分布式致动场，其整体形态由空间致动模式而非均匀加压控制。

Result: 实验证明1×1、2×2和3×3阵列具有可扩展的位移和力生成能力，并具有可重复的循环性能。在3×3×3阵列中选择性致动单胞，无需改变硬件即可产生弯曲和定向抓取等不同整体变形模式。此外，将主动与被动单胞耦合，可实现弯曲驱动的爬行运动，展示了异质阵列通过不对称变形实现平移的能力。

Conclusion: 确立了单胞级致动作为基于晶格的软机器人实现分布式变形的策略，并为可扩展、整体式的机器人架构奠定了基础。

Abstract: Soft lattice structures are increasingly used in robotics to tailor compliance and guide deformation; however, actuation is typically introduced at the device or module level, with actuators inserted into otherwise passive architectures. In this work, we move actuator-lattice co-design to the unit-cell scale. We present an embedded pneumatic unit cell that integrates curved-strut lattice geometry with a bidirectional bellow actuator within a single monolithic element. When tessellated, the lattice functions as a distributed actuation field in which global morphology is governed by spatial actuation patterns rather than uniform pressurization. Experimental characterization of 1x1, 2x2, and 3x3 tessellations demonstrates scalable displacement and force generation with repeatable cyclic performance.
